  - **R4 gap (later prompts and P32):** Bun 1.4.3 doesn't write an empty `trustedDependencies` into bun.lock, so `bun pm untrusted` and `bun ci` fall back to Bun's built-in trusted list (`bun pm default-trusted`, 367 names, e.g. esbuild, sharp).
    - In this tree only esbuild has an install script, and Bun's own script optimiser skips it anyway. Checked with a fresh clone: `package.json`, `bun.lock` and `bunfig.toml` copied to an empty folder, then `bun ci --verbose`. It printed `[Lifecycle Scripts] ignoring esbuild lifecycle scripts` (×3) and `… ignoring sharp lifecycle scripts`, and all three `esbuild/bin/esbuild` files stayed the 9181–9351-byte JS shim.
    - Before adding any package that is on that list and has an install script, run `bun install --verbose`, look for `[Lifecycle Scripts]` / `Blocked` / `Starting scripts` lines, and ask the owner.
    - P30 and P32 re-check the fresh-clone result on Vercel and in CI.
  - **Node on this Mac (before P01):** nvm has v24.12.0, v24.18.0 and v24.22.0, with the default alias `24` pointing at v24.22.0.
    - The Claude app session that ran P00 was started while v24.18.0 was the default, so its shell (and any terminal tab it opens) still has `~/.nvm/versions/node/v24.18.0/bin` first on PATH. There, `bun run smoke:compat` correctly fails with "expected Node >= 24.21 on the 24 line".
    - Fix: quit and reopen the Claude app, or start P01 from a new terminal, so `node -v` prints v24.22.0. For one session, the alternative is to put `~/.nvm/versions/node/v24.22.0/bin` first on PATH.
    - Non-interactive login shells resolve Homebrew Node 25.8.1 (`/opt/homebrew/bin/node`) before nvm.
    - The smoke's guard accepts Node 24.x at 24.21 or later only; Node 25 and Bun are rejected.

### Simplify pass over P00–P02 ✅ (2026-10-17)
> Owner request before P03: "run simplify on the entire project to date". Four reviewers (reuse, simplification, efficiency, altitude) read every tracked source file; the fixes below change no copy and no behaviour, except the TabBar height (a spec fix).
- Done:
  - **Shell:** `src/components/layout/public-shell.tsx` (a plain module: `Bar`, `PublicNavBar`, `PublicShell`, `MessageBody`) and `error-body.tsx` (`ErrorBody`, the 05 §10 error copy written once). The `(public)` layout, the root `not-found.tsx`, `error.tsx` and `global-error.tsx` use them, and `gutter.ts` adds `SHELL_MAIN`. NavBar's public variant renders `PublicNavBar`. Public pages, the 404 and the error view no longer load the creator Menu code.
  - **Primitives:**
    - Button renders its two-layer loading grid only when it has a `loadingLabel`; an icon button swaps its icon for the spinner.
    - Disclosure's trigger and the landing "Sign in" are `Button tertiary` (`asChild`) instead of copied classes. CountPill uses `pillVariants`, and StatusPill's dot is one lookup.
    - One `useFieldControl` hook gives TextField, TextArea, SelectField, Checkbox and Switch their id and ARIA wiring (`FieldProps` is exported; `describedBy` is gone).
    - CopyLinkField has one status state. CountdownChip formats its tooltip once per expiry and skips renders when nothing visible changed. FactList uses `gap` instead of negative margins.
  - **TabBar:** sized from `--tabbar-h` (56 px + safe area, with its border and `safe-area-bar` padding inside; tabs are 45 px), which is the space the root scroll padding and the toast offset reserve; it was 67 px. It renders its own in-flow spacer, so hosts don't pad for it (the gallery's `pb-[calc(…)]` is gone).
  - **Lib:**
    - `src/lib/time.ts` (`MINUTE_MS`, `HOUR_MS`, `DAY_MS`) replaces five local copies.
    - `format.ts` builds its date formatters once (UTC day and time at module level; deadline formats cached per zone, and an unknown zone falls back to UTC without being cached), replacing `safeZone`.
    - `LICENSE_V1_UPDATED_LABEL` uses `formatPostDate` (en-GB would print "Sept" for September). `LICENSE_V1_DISPLAY_TITLE` is the one source for the License v1 title (page h1, metadata, the Terms link).
    - `mailtoLink`, `urlLink` and `GOOGLE_PERMISSIONS_URL` live in `legal/types.ts`. The legal page builds React keys with one `keyed()` (the JSON of the item) instead of three key helpers.
  - **`env.ts`:** `ENV_KEYS` comes from the schema shape (`Object.keys(shape)`), so a new key can't be missed. One `secret` schema, `ADMIN_EMAILS` `.default([])`, no redundant `.int()`, and each issue's key is computed once. A new unit test checks that `.env.test` lists every `ENV_KEYS` entry (a missing key would let `next start` fill it from `.env.local` during e2e).
  - **Tests and config:** `format.test.ts` folds two one-off tests into its tables; `license-v1.test.ts` uses `licenseV1Tokens`; the e2e spec has one `visit()` helper and checks the Terms sections inside the legal loop (one page load fewer per project); `playwright.config.ts` sets `fullyParallel: true`; `biome.json` drops a `files.includes` list that `.gitignore` already covers. The gallery uses `SHELL_MAIN`, a `Frame` helper and a 12-country list.

### P04 — Passwordless sign-in and route protection ✅ (2026-10-18)
> 120 min · Depends on: P03 · Record: both admins in `ADMIN_EMAILS`, owner first (OQ-02 answer), what `user.name` holds after an OTP sign-up, the session helper names chosen

### Simplify pass over P04b–P05 ✅ (2026-10-18)
> Owner request after the P05 commit: "/simplify the last two commits". Four reviewers (reuse, simplification, efficiency, altitude) read the P04b and P05 diff; the fixes below change no copy and no behaviour.
- Done:
  - **Wrapping:** one `wrap-break-word` on the root `<body>` replaces six per-element `wrap-anywhere` classes (FactList values, the `/@handle` name and handle, Settings' handle line, the availability line and link preview, field error text, sign-in's masked address); see Decisions.
  - **Smaller client pages:** `src/lib/validation/display-name.ts` holds `DISPLAY_NAME_MAX`, `DISPLAY_NAME_COPY` and `displayNameMessage` with no Zod, so the onboarding and Settings forms stop loading Zod (about 35 KB gzipped) for one number; `profile.ts` defines the display-name field once and the domain's `cleanDisplayName` uses that schema (no second hard-coded 60).
  - **Forms:** `useFormAction(action, initialState, beforeSubmit?)` (`src/components/ui/use-form-action.ts`) holds the "dispatch the action by hand so React 19 doesn't reset the form" rule once; both forms use it. The profile step drops redundant state (suggestions come from the action's answer, one `Check` state replaces two, the effect's own flag replaces the request counter, the name and the 18+ box are no longer controlled); Settings' name field is uncontrolled too.
  - **Shared pieces:** `fieldError` and `formText` in `src/lib/result.ts`; `PRIMARY_WIDTH` (`src/components/ui/primary-width.ts`, six copies) and `READING_COLUMN` (`gutter.ts`, two); the Sheet's safe-area gutter once; `NotFoundBody({ title })` serves the `/@handle` 404; `ToastOnMount({ message })` in `toast.tsx` replaces `payouts-returned-toast.tsx`; `RATE_DIMENSIONS`, `MINUTE_MS` and `PAYOUTS_STATUS_LABEL.active` replace re-spelled values; the two action schemas are module constants.

### P06 — Connect a mock social account (OAuth plumbing) ✅ (2026-10-18)
> 120 min · Depends on: P05 · Record: the mock seam and token format decisions, the `extraScopes` / `requestedScopes` plumbing, `findPostByLinkKey` on the mock
- Done:
  - **Owner answers (in chat, 2026-10-18; Q-12–Q-15 below):** Q1 a: `getAuthorizeUrl` takes an optional `mockSubject`, which the start route passes (the session user id) for the mock only; real adapters ignore it. Q2 a: `error=provider_error` reads "Couldn't reach the platform. Try again." *(proposed)*. Q3 a: `social_account.scopes` stores only the scopes we asked for **and** were granted. Q4 a: `missing_scope` shows its message only; the Connect rows below are the retry (P08 revisits the "Try again" action). **Recorded answers, not asked:** OQ-15 (several accounts per platform, no cap), OQ-24 (Instagram view counts are an optional permission; only the plumbing is built here), OQ-33 (posting links are matched by id: every provider has `findPostByLinkKey`, every post a `linkKey`).
  - **Token cipher** `src/server/crypto/token-cipher.ts`: `createTokenCipher(keyB64)` (`kid`, `encrypt`, `decrypt`, `deriveSubkey`), `TokenCipherError` (`reason`: `bad_key` · `malformed` · `unknown_key` · `auth_failed`; the message never holds the payload), and the env-bound `encryptSecret(plaintext, aad)`, `decryptSecret(payload, aad)` and `deriveSubkey(info)` (HKDF-SHA256, 32 bytes). Format `v1.<kid>.<iv>.<tag>.<ct>` (base64url; `kid` = first 8 hex of sha256(key)); AES-256-GCM, 12-byte IV, 16-byte tag. One key ([H7] adds rotation); no `age-block` subkey (07 D93).
  - **Social contracts** `src/server/social/`: `types.ts` (02 §5.3 with `linkKey`, `optionalScopes`, `extraScopes`, `findPostByLinkKey`, and Q1's `mockSubject`; `SocialPostKind` / `PostVisibility` alias the values.ts types), `errors.ts` (`SocialAuthError`, `SocialPermissionError`, `SocialRateLimitError(retryAfterSec)`, `SocialUpstreamError`, `AlreadyLinkedError`; fixed-code messages), `registry.ts` (`enabledPlatforms()` in the UI order, `getProvider(platform)` → null when unknown or disabled, `redirectUriFor(p)` = `${APP_URL}/api/connect/${p}/callback`, plus the injectable `createSocialRegistry(env, adapters)`; enabling a platform without an adapter fails at the first import with "adapter not built yet (P27–P29)"), `http.ts` (`fetchJson` for P27+: timeout, Zod, 401/429/5xx mapping, retries 250 ms · 1 s · 4 s + ≤ 20 % jitter, messages without query or body), `pkce.ts` (`createPkcePair`, `pkceChallenge`), `oauth-state.ts` (`OAUTH_COOKIE = 'cliplease_oauth'`, `sealOAuthState`, `openOAuthState` (null when missing, tampered, malformed, from the future or older than 600 s), `stateMatches` (constant time), `oauthCookieOptions(appUrl)`, `parseReturnTo`), `tokens.ts` (`sealTokenColumns`, `tokenUpdateColumns`, `storeTokens`, AADs `social_account.access_token:<id>` / `social_account.refresh_token:<id>`), `link.ts` (`linkAccount` → `{ socialAccountId, created }`, `revokeQuietly`).
  - **Mock seam and token format (07 D36, D53, D68; Q1 a):** account suffix = the first 8 hex of sha256(user id), plus `_2` for `switch=1`, or `shared` under `shared_account`; `mockAccountIdFor(userId, second?)` (`seed_user_maya` → `mock_acct_c4180ab2`). Code `mock_code_<suffix>.<challenge>`; tokens `mock_access_<suffix>_<16 hex>` / `mock_refresh_…` (the seed's `mock_access_c4180ab2_seed` parses). Account `mock_acct_<suffix>`, `demo.creator` (`_2`: `demo.creator.2`, "Demo Creator 2", `mock://avatar/02`); posts `mock_post_<suffix>_NN`, `linkKey` = that id, permalink `https://example.com/mock/<id>`, thumbnails `mock://thumb/NN`. 24 posts from research §8 (post 01 = 14 s at 720 × 1280), 12 per page; `findPostByLinkKey` delegates to `getPost` (ownership checked); all eight scenarios, switched in tests with `setMockScenarioForTests` (test runs only). `mock-data.ts`: `MOCK_ANCHOR`, `MOCK_THUMB_TONES` (04 §2.5), `mockThumbnailSvg`, `MOCK_POSTS`.
  - **`extraScopes` / `requestedScopes` plumbing (07 D83):** the start route's `?insights=1` sets `extraScopes` = the provider's `optionalScopes` (none for the mock); the sealed cookie carries `requestedScopes` = required + extra; the callback refuses a connect missing a required scope (`missing_scope`) and stores granted ∩ requested (Q3 a); an optional scope that wasn't granted is not an error.
  - **Routes** `src/app/api/connect/[provider]/{start,callback}/route.ts` (02 §7.2, 07 D37): start → sign-in when there is no creator, 404 for an unknown or disabled platform, then state, PKCE, the sealed cookie (`HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/api/connect; Max-Age=600`, `Secure` only on https) and the redirect. Callback → the cookie is deleted on every outcome; a gate failure (no or bad cookie, another platform, a different state, another signed-in user) lands on `/dashboard/connections?error=state` before any exchange; afterwards every outcome goes back to the cookie's `returnTo` with `connected=<provider>` or `error=<code>` (typed from `CONNECT_ERROR_CODES`); unexpected errors land on Connections with `provider_error`. Nothing logs the code, a token or the cookie.

### P08 — Connections screen and account lifecycle (J2) ✅ (2026-10-19)
> 105 min · Depends on: P07 · Record: whether the owner added the platform marks to `public/brand/`; Disconnect leaves postings untouched (OQ-14 answer)
- Done:
  - **Owner answers (in chat, 2026-10-19; Q-17–Q-24 below):** Q-17 a: a running sync can't write anything back after a Disconnect (every sync write re-checks the account). Q-18 a: platform names as words; no mark files yet (`public/brand/` doesn't exist), added before P27/P28. Q-19 a: "Disconnecting…", "Disconnected @name.", "Back to Connections", and Reconnect reads "Connecting…" while it leaves. Q-20 a: one live video reads "1 live video came from @name. It stays live and keeps its Verified label, …". Q-21 a: `missing_scope` keeps no Try again (P06's Q-15 stands). Q-22 a: syncing shows the standard round spinner (LoaderCircle); RefreshCw only on the idle button. Q-23 a: P07's `sync-now-form.tsx` deleted. Q-24 a: one step with a bigger budget. **Recorded answers, not asked:** OQ-14 (Disconnect never touches `video` or `video_posting`: verified postings stay verified and read "· no longer linked"), OQ-15 (several accounts per platform, no cap).
  - **Account state and summaries:** `src/server/social/connection-state.ts` `deriveConnectionState(row, now)` → `{ syncProblem, syncing, blockedMinutes, stale }` (07 D02 / 05 §1.5 exactly; the 2-minute lease; minutes rounded up; > 24 h). `listAccountSummaries` returns 02 §5.4's full `AccountSummaryDTO` (`avatarSrc` = `/api/connections/avatars/{id}` when the platform gave one, `syncProblem`, `hasOlder`, `postCount` via `visiblePostsWhere`) plus the additive `accountType`, `connectedAt`, `syncing`, `blockedMinutes`, judged with the database's `now()` from the same query. `countLiveVideosFromAccount(a, accountId)`: published videos with a verified posting from the account (07 D72).
  - **Race guard (Q-17 a):** `sync.ts` runs the profile write and each page's upsert through `whileStillMine`: a short transaction that first re-reads the account `FOR SHARE` and stops the run (`RunStopped` → `not_found`, `needs_reconnect` or `already_syncing`) unless it is still `connected` and still holds this run's lease. Disconnect's wipe waits for a write in progress and then deletes what it wrote; a write that comes after the wipe finds the account disconnected and writes nothing.
  - **Disconnect (J2.14):** `src/server/social/disconnect.ts` `disconnectSocialAccount(accountId, actor)` → `{ revoked: 'revoked' | 'unsupported' | 'failed' }`, or null when the account isn't the creator's. An already disconnected row is a no-op (`unsupported`, no platform call). (1) The revoke runs before the wipe, outside any transaction: `getTokenSetForRevoke(accountId)` in `tokens.ts` (still the only module that decrypts) refreshes a connected account's expired token under the lock, returns null for an expired token on an account that must reconnect or on any failure, and never marks the row; a revoke that throws is logged by name and reported `failed`. (2) One transaction: the account wipe (03 §9.3's columns, one UPDATE so `social_account_disconnected_clean` passes; `WHERE status <> 'disconnected'`, so a concurrent Disconnect does nothing twice), the delete of posts nothing references, the scrub of referenced posts to `stale` with 03 §9.2's list, and the ids-only audit `social_account.disconnected` `{ socialAccountId, provider, revoked }`. `disconnectAccount({ socialAccountId })` in `_actions/social.ts` → `refresh()`.

Route `src/app/api/connections/avatars/[accountId]/route.ts`: no creator session → 401 `{ "error": { "code": "unauthorized" } }`; not a uuid → 404.

  - **Storage:** `index.ts` (the 02 §5.9 `ObjectStorage`; `storage()` by `STORAGE_DRIVER`, r2 throws "STORAGE_DRIVER=r2 arrives in P23"; `localDriver()`), `local.ts` (objects under `.data/storage/<key>`; multipart under `.data/storage/.multipart/{uploadId}/` with `manifest.json`, `{n}.part` and its MD5 in `{n}.etag`; part PUTs answer the quoted MD5 as `ETag`; `listParts` → the sorted parts or `'no_such_upload'` (no manifest, another key, or older than 24 h); `completeMultipart` checks R2's rules (ascending, every part but the last the same size and ≥ 5 MiB, ETags equal) and re-hashes each part while streaming it into the file, throwing `MultipartError('invalid_part' | 'no_such_upload')`; two completes of one upload either both write the same file or the later one answers `no_such_upload` once the parts are gone (the upload domain's fence settles them on one answer); the manifest is removed before the folder so a listing never sees half an upload; temp file + rename everywhere; keys must match the three prefixes with plain segments and resolve inside the root; upload ids are UUIDs), `local-url.ts` (signed URLs: HMAC-SHA256 under `deriveSubkey("local-storage-url")` over `method\nkey\nexp` + `ct\nlen` for a single PUT, `uploadId\npartNumber` for a part, `cd` for a GET; constant-time compare through the shared `src/server/crypto/hmac.ts`), `errors.ts`, `verify-object.ts` (`hasVideoSignature`, `hasJpegSignature`, `checkPartList`, `verifyUploadedObject`), `poster-token.ts` (`v1.<payload>.<sig>` under `deriveSubkey("poster-upload")`: videoId, creatorId, key, size, posterOrigin, exp).
  - **Dev route** `src/app/api/dev/storage/[...key]/route.ts` with `src/server/storage/dev-route.ts`: 404 unless `isDevStorageEnabled` (`STORAGE_DRIVER=local` and `isDevAppEnv(APP_ENV)`); 403 for a malformed, expired or wrong signature (segments carrying `/`, another kind's params, part numbers outside 1–10000); a part PUT streams at most `PART_BYTES` (413 over) and answers `ETag`; a single PUT needs the signed Content-Type (403) and exactly the signed length (413 over, 400 short) and never replaces an existing object (412; hard link); GET streams with the signed Content-Disposition and `no-store`; errors log the class only. `next.config.ts` keeps `/api/dev/storage/` out of `next dev`'s request log.
  - **Uploads** `src/server/storage/uploads.ts`: `isOriginalLocked(videoId, exec)` (D09, Q-39 a); `presignUpload` (original: the lock, then a new multipart on a new key and one row transaction under `FOR UPDATE` that re-checks the lock; the open upload is aborted and the previous object deleted after commit; `resume: true` returns the stored whole parts, or every part when the file is already assembled, `not_same_file` on another size or type, `upload_expired` past 24 h or gone; poster: a signed PUT for a new key with its exact type and size, plus the upload token; no row change), `presignParts` (≤ 20, 1…partCount, the row's own upload id), `completeUpload` (original: the server's `listParts` → `checkPartList` → `completeMultipart` with the server's ETags → `verifyUploadedObject` → a write fenced on status, upload id and key; parallel completes settle on one answer; a failed check deletes the object; poster: the token, then HEAD size + FF D8 FF, then the swap under `FOR UPDATE` and the old poster deleted), `abortUpload` (clears the file columns, aborts the upload, deletes any file assembled at the key; a ready original is untouched; allowed on a disabled video), `presignDownload`. Logs carry the video id and an error class only.
  - **Routes** `src/app/api/uploads/{start,parts,complete,abort}/route.ts` through `src/server/storage/upload-http.ts`: a creator session first (401 `{ error: { code: "unauthorized" } }`), `application/json` only, strict Zod (the start schema keeps `resume`; complete refuses `parts`/`etag`; display hints out of range become null), errors as `{ code }` with 400 · 404 · 409 · 410 (`upload_expired`) · 422, abort 204, `no-store` (the 401 body and `no-store` come from the shared `src/server/http.ts`, which the avatar and thumbnail image routes now use too); an unexpected error logs `uploads.route_failed` (class only) and answers 500 `{ code: "internal" }`.

- Verified:
  - **Pre-commit review (4 lenses: security, correctness, spec, tests; each finding checked by a second agent that tried to refute it):** 22 findings, 10 confirmed and fixed: an unexpected database or driver error in the upload routes reached Next's error log with the bound key, upload id and file name (now `uploads.route_failed` with the class only, 500 `{ code: "internal" }`); resume, abort and expiry could leave a fully assembled original at a key no row pointed to (they now delete it); a resume during a running complete could expire a good upload and make that complete delete it (resume now reports every part done when the file is assembled); an object deleted between HEAD and the read made complete answer 500 (now `not_found`); a parallel complete answered 404 or 410 where the other call had failed the file (now the stored code); `mimeFromFileName("x.constructor")` returned `Object` (now an own-property lookup); a listing that overlapped the folder removal returned half the parts (the manifest now goes first and is re-checked); and three missing tests (Q-38 on the poster path and a complete before any PUT; the D09 re-check inside the start's transaction; the route's 500 path). Refuted: an old poster token replayed against a newer one (write-once keys and the per-video key check), unreferenced posters filling storage (bounded by the signed length; P23 follow-up), a part PUT recreating a removed folder (it needs the manifest), exposing ETag over CORS (same origin), `..` inside a segment, and six test-coverage claims already covered. The tests themselves found two more races in the local driver (parallel completes answering `parts_incomplete`, fixed by queueing completes per upload; a parallel complete of a bad file answering `upload_expired`, fixed by answering as the call that ended the row).
  - **Simplify (owner request, 4 reviewers: reuse, simplification, efficiency, altitude):** fixed: the upload domain's four ways of ending an open upload (expire, expire-on-complete, settle-missing, finish-assembled) are one fenced `settle` step with the same answers and status codes; the lock's two `exists` reads are one `lockFlags` fragment shared by `isOriginalLocked` and the row read (start no longer reads the lock twice; the re-check inside the transaction stays); part math in one place (`partCount`, `expectedPartSize`) for the part check, resume, parts and start; `ORIGINAL_EXTENSION` replaces `originalExtension` and drives `mimeFromFileName` and the driver's content types; `posterKeyVideoId` replaces a copied regex; one HMAC helper (`src/server/crypto/hmac.ts`) for signed URLs and poster tokens; one 401 body and `no-store` header (`src/server/http.ts`) for the upload, dev storage and image routes; typed error codes in `upload-http.ts`; one dev-route handler for GET and PUT; the local driver assembles by streaming each part through one buffer instead of reading it whole, lists parts in parallel, shares one temp-then-rename helper, and drops its per-upload queue (a complete that loses the parts now answers `no_such_upload`, which the domain's fence already settles); clean-ups after a commit run in parallel; the test helpers send parts 4 at a time like the client, compare stored files by bytes instead of two MD5s, use a one-part crashed upload where three weren't needed, clean storage in one pass, and drop unused exports. Skipped: reusing `isUploadId` for the test tracker's uuid guard (it guards creator and video ids, so the name would mislead). Re-verified after: typecheck, lint, token lint, unit 47 files / 1,035 tests (storage and limits 272), `uploads` [int] 54 four runs in a row, full [int] 18 files / 242, full e2e 139 passed / 3 skipped, mutation checks unchanged (7 of 8; the same equivalent mutant), no `.tmp` or `.mutbak` left.
  - **Mutation checks** (each rule broken on purpose, then its tests run): dropping the owner scope (2 failed), the server's part-list check (1), the finish fence on upload id and key (1), the D09 lock (10), the local signature check (20), the dev route's `APP_ENV` guard (2) and the 24-hour expiry (4) each fail a test. Accepting a poster token signed for another creator fails none: the owner-scoped row read already refuses a video that isn't the caller's, so the token's creator check is defense in depth that no test can reach (an equivalent mutant).
  - `bun run typecheck` ✅ · `bun run lint` ✅ · `bun run test` ✅ (47 files, 1,035 tests; storage and limits 272, after simplify) · `bun run lint:tokens` ✅ (prints nothing) · `bun run test:int` ✅ (18 files, 242 tests; `uploads` 54, three runs in a row) · full `bun run test:e2e` ✅ 139 passed, 3 skipped (no UI changed; run for `next.config.ts`). One full [int] run failed P07's `refresh-lock` "10 concurrent calls make one refresh" once; it passed 4 of 4 alone and in the next full run (a timing flake, follow-up below).
  - Verify commands: `ffprobe … sample.mp4` → `stream|codec_name=h264|width=720|height=1280` · `format|duration=14.000000` ✅; `ls -l` → `sample.mp4` 166,229 B, `poster.jpg` 35,095 B ✅; the no-import `rg` prints nothing ✅; with `bun run dev`: the unsigned PUT → 403 ✅ and the four signed-out POSTs → 401 ✅; after the [int] runs `.data/storage` holds no `.tmp` file and no `.multipart` entry ✅; `grep -rn "console\." src/server/storage src/app/api/uploads src/app/api/dev` → nothing ✅; every non-test file under `src/server/storage/` starts with `import "server-only";` and `src/lib/upload/limits.ts` doesn't ✅; `bun run build` compiles the five new routes ✅.
  - Acceptance: the files and exports P10b greps for (`presignUpload`, `presignParts`, `completeUpload`, `abortUpload`, `isOriginalLocked`; `listParts` in `index.ts` and `local.ts`; `upload_expired`; `resume` in the start route's schema) ✅; the six constants with their exact values ✅; the 3-part upload leaves one object at `originals/<creatorId>/<videoId>/<uuid>.mp4`, no upload folder, and the row `ready`, 41,943,040 bytes, `video/mp4`, no upload id ✅; resume, expiry, abort, part-size and client-ETag cases ✅; size mismatch and bad bytes end `failed` with the error set and no object ✅; `original_locked` exactly per D09 ✅; unsigned or expired dev URLs → 403 and production or r2 → 404 (unit and route) ✅; no schema has a client-chosen key or ETag ✅; fixtures committed with the ffprobe output above ✅; no log line prints a key, upload id, URL or signature ✅.

    - the card could hide the focused control, and toasts drew over it;
    - focus fell to the page at every state change;
    - the hidden picker was an unlabeled tab stop in the done and error rows;
    - five test gaps: Cancel proven by no part PUT or complete after it; multi-part `uploadOriginal` unit tests; the card's running, Open and error states; the store's Q-41 and retry rules; the start body and hints.

    Refuted:
    - a cancelled file's frame becoming the cover (then made explicit: Cancel drops a frame on its way);
    - a failed Replace start hiding a ready original (the honest result of the creator's action);
    - an older pick's slow encode winning (needs a second picker round trip inside one encode);
    - `seekTime` 0 for an infinite duration (deliberate);
    - no drag-and-drop e2e (the prompt prescribes `setInputFiles`; dropping is a manual check).
  - **Simplify (owner request, 4 reviewers: reuse, simplification, efficiency, altitude; applied by three agents on disjoint files):**
    - One error-code list for server and browser.
    - Complete no longer waits for the frame capture.
    - The capture is skipped when the creator chose the cover, and on a retry after a stored frame.
    - The blank check does one pixel read.
    - The poster route makes one fewer query (creator join).
    - `getVideoForCreator` makes one query (lock flags in the select).
    - The slug retry uses `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` instead of a savepoint.
    - A single cover token, which also aborts an old frame's upload on Replace.
    - Shared `isUuid`, `notFoundNoStore`, `videoStepHref`, `useRefocusOnSwap`, `pressLocator`/`requestEnded`, `expectReadingColumn` and the integration helpers.
    - The dropzone's one wrapper, row and error button.
    - The File step's view derived in one place.
    - The CSS bottom stack.
    - The unused DTO fields (`source`, `ownership`, `originalBytes`) and duplicate tests are gone.

    Skipped:
    - The "up to 2 GB" literal stays in `upload-dropzone.tsx` (P10c's grep).
    - `posterError` and `connectDemo` stay (the prompt and infra §3 name them).
    - Splitting the store so dashboard pages don't load the upload code: about 10 KB minified, follow-up below.
    - A shared sha256-prefix helper: it would mean touching token-cipher and the mock provider.
  - **Mutation checks** (each rule broken on purpose, then its tests run): 13 of 13 caught. Each of these fails a test:
    - the poster route's owner check (2 failed);
    - the DTO's creator scope (1);
    - `resolveStep`'s gate (5);
    - the title tidy-up (7);

    - `bun run lint`;
    - `bun run lint:tokens` (prints nothing);
    - `bun run test` (57 files, 1,389 tests);

  - **Flakes seen once each and not reproduced:**

### P10c — Resumable original uploads: retry, resume and cancel ✅ (2026-10-20)
> 90 min · Depends on: P10b, P10a · Record: the question 1 answer and jitter formula; offline → Paused; Start again after a reload; which `lastModified` each e2e project reported; status line "Current prompt: P11 · Last verified: P10c"
- Done:
  - **Owner answers (in chat, 2026-10-20; Q-45–Q-49 below):**
    - **Q-45 a (the prompt's question 1):** `PART_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5` counts retries after the first send, so a part goes up to 6 times. The waits are 1, 2, 4, 8 and 16 s, each plus jitter `base + floor(random() × base × 0.25)` (`retryDelayMs`). Paused comes after about 31–39 s of failures.
    - **Q-46 a:** two Paused boxes.
      - Same tab: "Upload paused at {n}%. Check your connection.". An offline pause shows only Cancel and carries on by itself on `online`. A pause after the retries ran out shows Resume + Cancel, and it also carries on by itself once back online.
      - After a reload or a closed tab: "Upload paused at {n}%. Choose the same file to continue." with Choose file + Cancel.
    - **Q-47 a:** the floating card.
      - Retrying keeps "Uploading {title}" and adds "Connection lost. Retrying…".
      - Paused shows "Upload paused at {n}%" with the title, Open and ×. It goes back to Uploading when the upload carries on.
      - Screen readers hear "Connection lost. Retrying…" and "Upload paused at {n}%." on the page, and "Upload paused at {n}%: {title}" from the card.
    - **Q-48 a:** one bigger step.
    - **Q-49 b** (the owner's pick over the recommended "known limit"): a second tab on the same video shows "This video is uploading in another tab." in place of the drop area, and refreshes when the other tab's upload ends.
    - **Q-50 b** (asked after the simplify pass; the owner's pick over the recommended follow-up): Cancel's abort names the upload it ends, so a late abort can't end a newer upload, and a start right after a Cancel no longer waits.
    - **Q-51 b:** delete the 107 orphaned upload folders P10b's runs left in `.data/storage/.multipart/`.
  - **Pure modules (`src/lib/upload/`):**
    - `retry.ts`:
      - `RETRY_DELAYS_MS` and `retryDelayMs`.
      - `sendPartWithRetry`:
        - 404 → gone. 400/413 and other 4xx (except 403/408/429) → failed.
        - Offline spends no retry: it waits for `online`.
        - One re-sign per part after a network error or a 403.
      - `withRetries` (part URLs and complete), `wait`, `abortError`, `isAbort`.
    - `resume.ts`:
      - The hint `cliplease.upload.<videoId>` (Zod-checked; a bad value is removed).
      - `matchesResumeHint` (name, size, lastModified), `planResume`, `keepsResumeHint`, `OriginalUploadProgress`.
    - `wake-lock.ts` `holdScreenWakeLock`.
    - `device.ts` `isIosDevice`.
    - `tab-presence.ts`: `announceUploads`, `useUploadElsewhere` and `onOtherTabUploads`, over BroadcastChannel `cliplease.uploads`.
    - `file-step-state.ts` `deriveFileStep`: the File step's state as one tagged union, table-tested.
  - **Browser upload:**
    - `uploadOriginal` keeps its signature and callers. It adds `resume`, `expectedUploadId`, `onStatus`, `onStarted`, `hints` and `network`.
      - Start is never aborted.
      - Another upload id than this device's → `stale_upload`.
      - The hint is written once start answers, unless Cancel came first.
      - Only the missing parts are sent, and offline workers start no part.
      - An upload gone at its parts goes straight to complete, which maps the outcome.
      - `parts_incomplete` gets one repair pass.
    - `abortOriginalUpload(videoId, uploadId)` sends a `keepalive` POST `/api/uploads/abort` naming the upload (Q-50); Cancel has already cleared the hint when pressed. At its end, `uploadOriginal` clears only its own upload's hint, and none if its start never answered.
  - **Store (Q-42) and card:**
    - **Phases and guards:**
      - The phases gain retrying and paused (`pausedBy` offline · retries), plus `uploadId` and `isLive`.
      - beforeunload and the Screen Wake Lock are on only while uploading, retrying or checking.
    - **Restarts:**
      - Back online resumes an upload whose retries ran out.
      - Resume resumes the open upload, and so does Try again after `network`, `internal` or `unauthorized`.
      - A same-tab restart of the same file reuses its metadata read.
    - **Cancel:**
      - It shows rest at once and clears the hint.
      - It waits up to 15 s for a start still answering, then sends `/abort` once, naming the upload (Q-50): this tab's own (even one a start opened after the Cancel), or the one a reload left paused.
      - A start right after a Cancel waits for the cancelled start's answer, at most 15 s (the abort's own clock). Answering later, that start would replace the new upload on the server. It also waits for the abort, at most 15 s more, but only when the abort went out unnamed. A named abort is never waited on.
    - **Other tabs:** this tab's ended entry for a video goes when another tab announces that video.
    - **Card:** shows Retrying and Paused (Q-47).
  - **Server:**
    - `getOriginalUploadProgress` is owner-scoped, read-only and selects only the columns it needs:
      - null unless the row is `uploading` with a key, upload id and size;
      - `expired` past 24 h;
      - otherwise the whole parts stored.
    - It shares `inspectOpenUpload` with P10a's `resumeOriginal`, which covers fresh → parts, assembled or expired.
    - A storage error → null plus `uploads.progress_failed`.
    - The video page passes the result to the File step: the upload id and bytes only.
    - `/api/uploads/abort` takes an optional `uploadId` (1–1024 characters; Q-50). `abortUpload` ends only that upload. If it names an upload that isn't open any more, it answers 204 and changes nothing.
  - **File step:**
    - The Retrying line.
    - Paused here or after a reload: the bar frozen, plus a warning Banner (`PausedBox`).
    - Mismatch: "This doesn't look like the same file. Start again?" with Start again and Choose another file.
    - Expired, Not finished and the other-tab line.
    - The iPhone hint "On iPhone, choose the video from Files for full quality." on iOS.
    - Milestones at 25/50/75 %, counted from where a resume starts.
    - The dropzone's notice is a render prop that is given "open the picker".
  - **Tests:**
    - unit:
      - new: retry, resume, wake-lock, device, tab-presence and file-step-state;
      - `uploadOriginal`, the store and messages extended;
      - shared fakes in `tests/helpers/upload-fakes.ts`.
    - [int] `tests/integration/upload-progress.test.ts` (whole parts only, 25 h → expired, aborted, another creator, ready, assembled, gone, storage error).
    - e2e `tests/e2e/upload-resume.spec.ts` (a 40 MiB file written once per worker):
      - a part retried through a re-signed URL; reload → paused; a different file → Mismatch; the same file → only part 2 sent → attached;
      - Cancel while retrying, plus the iPhone hint;
      - offline;
      - the Q-47 card;
      - Q-49's two tabs;
      - after a reload, Cancel and Start again on the paused box name the paused upload (Q-50).
    - `upload.spec.ts`: Cancel now ends at rest after a reload.
    - Shared e2e helpers: `DRAFT_TITLE`, `AT_REST`, `uploadCard`, `cancelAndWaitForAbort`, `abortAnswered`, `multipartDir`, `isPartPutRequest`, `trackPartPuts`, `isOriginalStart`, `chooseOriginal`, `holdUploadRoute` (was `holdPartUrls`). The [int] helpers gained `bytesOf` and `captureLogEvents`.
- Verified:
  - **Pre-commit review (5 lenses: correctness, server-security, spec, UX/a11y, tests; a second agent tried to refute each finding):** 22 findings, 17 confirmed (deduplicated into 9 fixes), 5 refuted. Fixed:
    - Try again after network/internal/unauthorized resumes instead of starting from 0 %.
    - Presence asks every second while watched, and a closing tab says so (pagehide), so a hidden uploading tab is not dropped.
    - A stale entry no longer lifts the other-tab lock.
    - A hint is kept when the server's progress is unknown.
    - The offline Cancel's abort is not re-sent later.
    - A resume's bar starts from the server's bytes, and a pause shows the latest bytes.
    - Choosing a file for a paused upload checks it first.
    - Mismatch is keyed, announced, and focuses "Choose another file".
    - The helper line is hidden while another tab uploads.

    Refuted:
    - Cancel telling other tabs too early.
    - The repair pass showing 100 %.
    - The offline pause dropping beforeunload.
    - An early Cancel leaving the hint; the simplify pass made it explicit anyway.
    - Missing tests for File step states; they are now covered by the `deriveFileStep` table.
  - **Simplify (owner request; 4 reviewers: reuse, simplification, efficiency, altitude; applied in stages on disjoint files, then a before/after behaviour review by 3 finders and 3 skeptics):**
    - Applied:
      - One copy of the wait/abort helpers.
      - `withRetries` moved next to `sendPartWithRetry`; it now also waits for `online` after a retry wait.
      - The hint rules (`keepsResumeHint`) live in `resume.ts`.
      - Simpler outcomes in `sendParts`.
      - The hint is not written back after a Cancel during start.
      - `inspectOpenUpload` shared, with a narrower progress select.
      - Presence asks only while a File step watches.
      - The store drops ended entries on another tab's announce, replacing the File step effect.
      - `isLive`; one `resume` option; no `guarding` or `pausedSent`.
      - The metadata read is reused for the same file once it got the video's size.
      - The File step derivation became the pure `deriveFileStep`.
      - `PausedBox`, the notice render prop, `pausedPercent` beside `uploadPercent`, and one "Upload paused at" builder.
      - `useClientValue` (also used by CopyLinkField).
      - Shared test fakes and e2e/[int] helpers; the 40 MiB file is written once per worker; the iPhone-hint test was folded into the Cancel test; five progress tests store no parts.
    - The behaviour review confirmed two issues, both fixed:
      - A Cancel's key could come back after another tab's upload (it now clears on the first server change).
      - A dropped test row: expired with no hint, and expired before the hint is read.
    - Skipped:
      - Per-video store hooks and `memo(PosterPicker)` (a P10b pattern; follow-up).
      - Web Locks presence (optional; WebKit across pages unproven).
      - Streaming the progress read (the box would shift).
      - `uploadErrorLine`'s unreachable `paused` case (kept, like `canceled`, for the exhaustive table).
  - **Q-50 review (2 finders, 2 skeptics) of the named abort:**
    - 4 confirmed and fixed:
      - A start right after a Cancel could race the cancelled start on the server. It now waits for that start's answer (the second simplify pass put this wait on the abort's own 15 s clock).
      - A fresh run's Cancel could name the page's older paused upload. It now names that upload only when this tab holds none of its own.
      - A cancelled run's last clean-up could remove a newer upload's hint. It now clears only its own upload's hint.
      - No e2e covered the paused box's Cancel or Start again. A new test covers both: it holds the Start again abort until the new start has answered, and the new file still uploads to the end.
    - 2 refuted:
      - A named abort leaving a failed row as it is: harmless, and now said in the comment.
      - The 15 s Retrying allowance hiding a product delay: no product delay is involved; the comment was corrected.
    - Added: a named abort from another creator gets the same 404.
  - **Second simplify pass (owner request, 4 reviewers: reuse, simplification, efficiency, altitude):**
    - Applied:
      - `abortOriginalUpload` is the server call only; its hint clear never removed anything, since Cancel clears the hint when pressed.
      - A run that never heard back from start leaves the hint alone (it wrote none).
      - A start after a Cancel now waits on the abort's own clock (15 s) instead of a second 5 s one. It also waits for an unnamed abort, the one abort that could end it.
      - `within(promise, ms)` clears its timer.
      - The start is one async function.
      - One `uploadIdField` for the parts and abort routes.
      - Duplicate comments trimmed.
      - e2e:
        - shared `isOriginalStart`, `chooseOriginal`, `holdUploadRoute` and `abortAnswered`;
        - the refetch wait is one `expect.poll`;
        - the Q-50 test pauses at 0 % by failing the part-URL request: no part body sent, about 8 s instead of uploading about 120 MiB;
        - Cancel-while-retrying uses a 1 MiB file.
      - Unit fixtures: `saveHint` and a `resume` argument on the store test's `start()`.
    - Skipped:
      - A server-side guard on start order (needs a column and API changes; P23 follow-up).
      - Merging `Runtime.cancelled` into `running` (the explicit field reads clearer).
      - Returning the upload id from `uploadOriginal` instead of the store's `opened`: it would lose the id on the store's error path.
  - **Mutation checks** (each rule broken on purpose, then its tests run): 25 of 25 caught. Each of these fails a test:
    - no re-sign after a network error or 403 (9 failed);
    - one retry too many, Q-45 (3);
    - an offline failure spends a retry (4);

      - a run's last clear removes a newer upload's hint (1);

  - **Test runs, all ✅:**
    - `bun run typecheck` (0 errors), `bun run lint` (383 files), `bun run lint:tokens` (prints nothing).

After Q-50 and its review, `--repeat-each 3` gave 78 passed (the new test included), and 78 again after the second simplify pass.
    - `upload.responsive.spec.ts` on all four projects: 4 passed.
  - **Flakes found and fixed:**
    - In the Cancel tests: once in 48 runs, a WebKit Cancel test reloaded while the router's refetch body was still arriving, and WebKit logged "Failed to fetch RSC payload … Load failed". `cancelAndWaitForAbort` now waits until the page's resource timing shows that fetch ended. In Chromium, Playwright's `response.finished()` never settles for that streamed response and `body()` finds no data. After the fix, the Cancel tests passed `--repeat-each 4` (24 passed), then the full run and the ×3 run above.
    - In `upload.spec.ts`'s Cancel test on Chromium, the test took the first start to answer as the original's. Sometimes that was the cover frame's own start, which has no upload id. It now waits for the original's start.
    - On Chromium, with five 40 MiB uploads running in parallel, the first Retrying line sometimes took over 5 s, because the part bodies go through Playwright's interception. The first resume test's Retrying check now allows 15 s (`RETRYING_SHOWS_MS`); a real product delay would still fail. The Cancel-while-retrying test needs no allowance since it uses a 1 MiB file. The ×3 run then passed 72 of 72.
  - **`lastModified` per e2e project** (the first resume test records it):
    - chromium-desktop reports the file's exact milliseconds: the first pick, the second pick and the mtime are equal.
    - webkit-iphone rounds down to the second: both picks are equal, below the mtime by its milliseconds.
    - Both picks match within each project, so a resume matches.

    - Prove the same flow on R2 with a file of at least 120 MB.
    - R2's CORS must allow the part PUTs with progress (no Content-Type) and the keepalive abort is same-origin.
    - An expired R2 URL is an opaque 403, seen as a network error, which already triggers the re-sign.
    - Keep `/api/uploads/abort`'s optional `uploadId` (Q-50). R2's upload ids are opaque strings, which the 1–1024 limit (`uploadIdField`) allows.
    - Optional: a server-side guard on start order (a start that commits after a newer one refuses). It needs a column and API changes. It would cover other devices and the unnamed abort after 15 s, which the tab's own wait can't.
  - **P31b:** keep `screen-wake-lock` allowed in the Permissions-Policy.
  - **Performance:**
    - Per-video store hooks so the cover picker and the card don't re-render on every progress tick.
    - P10b's split of `upload-store.ts` from the layout's card.
  - **Later hardening:**
    - Wipe `cliplease.upload.*` at sign-out (D80 keeps them until the upload ends, is cancelled or expires).
    - Web Locks for presence, if instant release after a crash matters.
  - **Known limits:**
    - Next can't navigate client-side while fully offline (it does a full load), so navigating while offline ends the tab's upload; a reload then offers the paused resume.
    - An abort that doesn't reach the server is not re-sent; the next start or the 24 h window ends that upload.
    - A Cancel during a start that hasn't answered within 15 s sends its abort unnamed, which ends whatever upload the video has open.

### Review — P10–P11 code review and fixes ✅ (2026-10-23)
> Not a plan prompt: the owner ran `/code-review` on every P10 and P11 commit after P11c, then asked for all findings to be fixed (owner answers Q-82–Q-86). Nothing in P12's scope was started. Status line unchanged.
- Done:
  - **Review:** 10 angles over P10a–P11c (about 16,000 lines of production code; the upload server and routes, the Where posted actions, the posting domain and the step's client logic read in full): 9 findings, none urgent (most matter once real platform adapters exist, P27–P29).
  - **Owner answers (in chat, 2026-10-23; Q-82–Q-86 below, all option a):** one 45 s budget for a save's link checks, late links save as "We couldn't check this right now." (Q-82); unticking a stored same-video tick takes it back (Q-83); a link checked in the last 30 s keeps its result for Check again and Continue, Check again sooner says "Checked a moment ago. Try again in a few seconds." (Q-84); the length warning names the account when two verified posts share a platform (Q-85); the whole "Show buyers this link" rule moves into the server's save (Q-86).
  - **Fixed:** the shared deadline reaches every platform call (`getPost` / `findPostByLinkKey` take an optional `signal`; `verifyPostingLink(creatorId, link, { signal, refreshBy })`; a link the deadline stopped before any call saves as `check_failed` with no `checked_at`; a token refresh that couldn't finish in time isn't started, `NoTimeToRefreshError`); `withdrawSameVideo` and the Continue's take-back; the 30 s cooldown (`CheckedRecentlyError`; a save carries a recent check's columns over with no call); `confirmSameVideo` and `withdrawSameVideo` refuse a video that isn't a draft or turned off, under the lock (`PostingsLockedError`); the domain refuses "nothing chosen" (`InvalidPostingsError('nothing_chosen')`); an unpublished video's Continue goes to its page; the save returns the step facts it wrote (`{ ok, postings, facts }`), so the Continue doesn't read them again; the domain applies the whole show-link rule (the browser posts the Switch only once turned); the length warning names the account (Q-85).
  - **Review of the fixes:** one adversarial reviewer confirmed all 9 and found 2 small gaps in the new deadline (a link stopped during its database reads counted as checked; a token refresh could run past the budget), both fixed with tests.
- Verified: `bun run typecheck` ✅ · `bun run lint` ✅ (501 files) · `bun run lint:tokens` ✅ (prints nothing) · `bun run test` ✅ (110 files, 2738 tests) · `bun run test:int` ✅ (27 files, 483 tests; `postings.test.ts` 92, `videos-postings-actions.test.ts` 27, `refresh-lock.test.ts` 10) · `bun run test:e2e` ✅ (201 passed, 3 skipped by project as before, 3.1 min)
- Decisions: see Decisions (2026-10-23, Review).
- Open questions: none.

### Review — Faster tests and checks ✅ (2026-10-23)
> Not a plan prompt: before P12 the owner asked for a faster test setup that runs only what each change needs, then chose option b of the audit's safe wins (C1, C2, C3, C5, C6 and R1; owner answers Q-87–Q-89). Nothing in P12's scope was started. Status line unchanged.
- Done:
  - **C1, a local test database (Q-87, Q-89):** Postgres 18.6 (the `postgres:18` image; Neon runs 18.6) in Docker Desktop: container `cliplease-test-db` on 127.0.0.1:54329, fsync and synchronous commit off (it only ever holds test rows). `bun run test:db` (`scripts/test-db.sh`) opens Docker Desktop when Docker isn't up, creates or starts the container and migrates it. `.env.test.local` (git-ignored) points both test URLs at it; deleting it goes back to the Neon `test` branch. The test-database guard is unchanged.
  - **C2, the Step 0 shortcut (Q-88):** `scripts/check.sh`. `bun run check:full` runs typecheck, lint, token lint, unit, integration and every e2e spec, and records a green run in `.data/last-green` (a SHA-256 fingerprint of every tracked or new non-Markdown file, in byte order, plus HEAD and the time). `bun run check:step0 [specs]` always runs the fast checks; it skips integration and e2e when the fingerprint matches, else runs `test:int:changed` since the recorded commit and the given specs.
  - **C3, affected-only commands:** `bun run test:changed` and `bun run test:int:changed` (Vitest `--changed`, which follows imports). `forceRerunTriggers` (a change to one runs every test) add to Vitest's own defaults the files tests read without importing (`bun.lock`, `tsconfig.json`, `.env.example`, `drizzle/**`, `src/app/globals.css`, `tests/fixtures/**`) and the setup files with what only they import (`tests/setup/**`, `src/db/client.ts`, `tests/helpers/{db,read-latest-email,test-database,test-run-lock}.ts`).
  - **C5 and the routine:** AGENTS.md: one `bun run test:e2e a b c` per verify step (each one builds the app); Step 0, Step 3, Step 4 and §7 use the new commands.
  - **C6:** `/*turbopackIgnore: true*/` on the email and storage folder roots and on the storage driver's two path builders (4 comments; both drivers run only in development and test, 07 D51). The build traced the whole project before, with warnings, `.data` included (321 MB of test uploads in 7 routes); now no route traces anything from `.data` or `src`, and the build prints no warnings. Test emails older than 24 h are deleted when an integration or e2e run starts (14,076 files → about 1,400).
  - **R1:** 23 end-of-file database wipes removed (19 `afterAll(resetDb)`, and the `resetDb` in 4 `afterAll` blocks that also delete the file's stored files): every integration file already empties the database as it starts (`tests/setup/integration.ts`), so they only repeated it. Both runs now begin with `startTestRun` (`tests/helpers/test-run-lock.ts`): take the test-run lock, empty the database on the lock's own connection and delete old test emails, so nothing an interrupted run left reaches the next one. Kept: the postings suite's in-test reset, and the two "duplicate" tests, since P03's acceptance criteria require all nine constraint smoke cases and the no-date-of-birth schema check.
  - **Flaky tests found on the way (fixed):** the Library streams behind `loading.tsx`, so specs navigated or went offline before it finished (`waitForLibrary`: the URL and the h1); e2e handles had 2 random bytes and two collided in a full run (now 4); a test going offline cut Next's NavBar link prefetches (a new `{ offline: true }` expected failure, router RSC fetches only, used by the two offline tests).
  - **Review of the change set:** 5 reviewers and 5 skeptics; 13 findings confirmed and fixed, 12 refuted. Fixed: the fingerprint depended on the shell's locale and stopped `check:full` silently when the last listed file was deleted; `--changed` missed the setup files' own helpers and two files unit tests read (`globals.css`, `.env.example`); `test:db` gave no message when Postgres never got ready; the database emptying didn't share the lock's guards (now on the lock's connection); the storage traces above; the longer handles made onboarding's "unknown creator" link an invalid handle, so that test checked the format refusal instead of a lookup that finds nothing (now `/@no.such.<17 characters>`, valid); a sign-in safeguard I had added was unneeded and is gone.
  - **`/simplify` (4 cleanup reviewers: reuse, simplification, efficiency, altitude):** one truncate, `emptyPublicTables` (`tests/helpers/test-database.ts`), for `resetDb` and the run start (the two copies had already drifted: only one said "run db:migrate" on an empty schema); `startTestRun` replaces the lock call in both global setups and takes in the email pruning (the one-function housekeeping file and the `{ empty }` option are gone); `pruneOldEmails` lives in `read-latest-email.ts`, which owns the folder, and reads the time from the file names (`DAY_MS`); `forceRerunTriggers` spreads `configDefaults`; `check.sh` has one `fast()` and reads HEAD once; `test-db.sh` drops a check `docker start` makes needless; the unknown-creator link is a fresh `uniqueHandle`; 14 blank lines left in import blocks are gone. The efficiency reviewer found nothing worth changing (a fingerprint takes 0.07 s). Skipped: a route-agnostic "follow a link and wait for its loading skeleton to go" helper (reaches Connections call sites outside this work; worth doing when another streamed page needs it); a `git write-tree` fingerprint (Vitest's `--changed` compares against HEAD anyway, so it buys no exact base); collision-free e2e ids from the worker index (four random bytes already make a repeat negligible); running the fast checks at once (1.5 s for mixed output); skipping the e2e build's own type pass (3.8 s a run, but it needs a `next.config.ts` switch: the owner's call).
- Verified: `bun run test:db` ✅ (Docker Desktop, Postgres 18.6, migrated; again in 1.3 s) · `bun run check:full` ✅ in 2 min 40 s after `/simplify`: `typecheck` ✅ · `lint` ✅ (501 files) · `lint:tokens` ✅ (prints nothing) · `test` ✅ (110 files, 2738 tests, 6 s) · `test:int` ✅ (27 files, 483 tests, 40 s) · `test:e2e` ✅ (201 passed, 3 skipped by project as before, 1.8 min; the build prints no warnings) · `bun run check:step0` on that code ✅ (10 s; integration and e2e skipped) · two integration runs in shuffled file order ✅ (seeds 1234, 98765: 27 files, 483 tests) · the two offline e2e tests ×8 on chromium-desktop and webkit-iphone ✅ (32 passed) · no `.next/server/**/*.nft.json` lists `.data/` or `src/` · `.data/emails` keeps only the last day's emails ✅
- Decisions: see Decisions (2026-10-23, Tests).
- Open questions: none.

| 2026-10-17 | P02 | Component APIs:<br>- **Button:** variants primary, secondary, tertiary, link and destructive; sizes lg, sm (with `hit-44`) and icon. The icon size ignores `variant` (its type forbids one and requires `aria-label`): a 44 px `fill` circle with an `ink` icon, `hover:brightness-95` and `active:scale-98`, so no variant's hover or pressed background can hide the icon. `loading` / `loadingLabel` stack the label and the spinner layer in one grid cell, so the width never changes; the hidden layer is aria-hidden and never repeats the children. The type requires `loadingLabel` whenever `loading` is passed (lg and sm), because that label is the button's name while loading. Press feedback runs at 100 ms (`active:duration-100`, 04 §6 `--dur-press`); colors at 200 ms. `asChild` uses Radix Slot. `link` has no pill box: no padding or min-width, `min-h-11` (lg), regular weight, and it wraps (`whitespace-normal text-left`) so long Contents links never scroll sideways.<br>- **TextField:** takes a `prefix` (from `CURRENCY_DISPLAY[c].prefix`) in place of React's RDFa `prefix`. The prefix span stays aria-hidden and its id comes first in `aria-describedby`, so screen readers hear the currency once. The read-only look (`fill`, no border) applies to TextField and TextArea only, because a `<select>` always matches `:read-only`. Errored fields keep the `danger` border on hover.<br>- **Checkbox:** the whole ≥ 44 px row is the `<label htmlFor>`, wrapping the box and the text.<br>- **Menu:** no MenuSeparator (04 names no separators) and no `outline-none` on items (04 §3.3), so the global ring shows.<br>- **StatusPill:** `tone` plus `dot` (`solid` / `hollow`, neutral only). Awaiting and danger always show their dot; cleared shows a 12 px check.<br>- **CountdownChip:** `expiresAt` (ISO), `srPrefix` ("Waiting for you") and `tone="neutral"` (the buyer's `/r`).<br>- **ConfirmSheet:** the safe button is AlertDialog.Cancel. The header has the same 44 px Close control as Sheet (a second Cancel), so initial focus is set explicitly to the safe button in `onOpenAutoFocus` rather than left to Radix's single cancel ref. The confirm variant is `destructive` by default, or `primary` / `secondary` for TurnOffAutoRenew and price sheets. With `cancelVariant="primary"` (TurnOffAutoRenewSheet) the safe button comes first in the footer, so the primary is first on mobile and last (right) on desktop. `confirmLoading` requires `confirmLoadingLabel`.<br>- **Stepper:** `steps`, `current` (0-based); mobile layout below md.<br>- **DataTable:** `columns` / `rows` / `rowKey`.<br>- **Avatar:** `name` (initials) and optional `src`. | 04 §9.2–§9.31; agent (stated) |

| 2026-10-20 | P10c | Owner answers in chat after the simplify pass (Q-50 b, Q-51 b). Q-50: Cancel's abort names its upload. This is a P10a contract change for P23: `/api/uploads/abort` takes `{ videoId, uploadId? }` (`uploadId` 1–1024 characters, else 400 invalid). `abortUpload` ends only that upload, and one that isn't open any more answers 204 and changes nothing. Without `uploadId` the abort behaves as before. On the client, `abortOriginalUpload(videoId, uploadId)` is the server call only. `clearResumeHint(store, videoId, uploadId?)` lets a run clear only its own upload's hint. `uploadOriginal` tells `onStarted` even when a Cancel came while start answered, so that Cancel can name the upload. The store no longer waits for a named abort. A start right after a Cancel waits for the cancelled start's answer (15 s at most), so that start can't replace the new upload. | Q-35 | A missing or short part at complete: keep the upload open or end it | Keep it open: 422 `parts_incomplete`, the client re-sends | P10a | answered (P10a): the default |
| Q-36 | Extra safety on the dev storage route | Size caps, the exact type and length signed into a single PUT, write-once objects (signed URLs kept out of the dev log either way) | P10a | answered (P10a): the default |
| Q-37 | Two Step 0 checks that fail only by wording (a comment naming `original_pending_key`; `STORAGE_DRIVER`'s default lives in `.env.example`) | Continue and record them; no code change | P10a | answered (P10a): the default |
| Q-38 | A video disabled while its file uploads | Refuse parts and complete (404); abort still allowed | P10a | answered (P10a): the default |
| Q-39 | An expired checkout that never got its expiry notice and the original lock | Still locks (D09 literal) until the clean-up job arrives | P10a | answered (P10a): the default |
| Q-40 | New copy for the title page and the File step (title errors, Back target, helper, live-region words, cover alt text, "Upload didn't finish…", "Add a cover image for buyers.", two cover-picture errors) | Use them as written, recorded as proposed copy | P10b | answered (P10b): the default |
| Q-41 | Replace when the creator chose their own cover | Keep the chosen cover; take a new frame only when there is no cover or it is a frame | P10b | answered (P10b): the default |
| Q-42 | Tapping another tab while the video uploads | Ask first ("Leave this page? Your upload will stop.") | P10b | answered (P10b): the owner's own answer: don't stop the upload; it carries on across the app's pages with a floating card (progress, then complete or stopped) whose Open goes back to the File step. Follow-up, both the default: the card floats above the TabBar below lg and bottom-right from md; its words "Uploading {title}", "Checking the file…", "Upload complete", "Upload didn't finish", "Open", and "Keep this tab open until the upload finishes. You can use other pages meanwhile." under the drop area |
| Q-43 | Odd characters in titles | Tidy them: line breaks and tabs → spaces, runs squeezed, invisible and direction marks removed | P10b | answered (P10b): the default |
| Q-44 | P10b runs 2–3× its 2-hour estimate | One step with a bigger budget (as P08, P09) | P10b | answered (P10b): the default |
| Q-45 | What `PART_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5` counts (the prompt's question 1) | Five retries after the first send, waits 1/2/4/8/16 s plus jitter | P10c | answered (P10c): the default |
| Q-46 | The words when the connection drops with the page still open | Two boxes: "Check your connection." with Resume in the same tab, "Choose the same file to continue." after a reload | P10c | answered (P10c): the default |
| Q-47 | The floating card when an upload retries or pauses | "Connection lost. Retrying…" under "Uploading {title}"; "Upload paused at {n}%" with the title, Open and ×; the screen-reader lines | P10c | answered (P10c): the default |
| Q-48 | P10c runs 2–3× its 90-minute estimate | One step with a bigger budget | P10c | answered (P10c): the default |
| Q-49 | The same video open in two tabs at once | Accept it as a known limit and write it down | P10c | answered (P10c): option b, detect the other tab and show "This video is uploading in another tab." |
| Q-50 | Should Cancel's abort name the upload it ends (a late abort can end a newer upload)? | Leave it as a noted follow-up | P10c | answered (P10c): option b, do it now |
| Q-51 | 109 leftover upload folders (107 orphaned from P10b's runs, 2 paused from manual checks) | Leave them | P10c | answered (P10c): option b, delete the 107 orphaned folders |
| Q-52 | A refused connect gives the new tokens back, which on Google and TikTok also ends the live account's grant | Give them back only when no live account holds that platform account | Review | answered (Review): option b, the default |
| Q-53 | Which review findings to fix | The 15 reported first | Review | answered (Review): option b, all 99, with these questions asked first |
| Q-54 | Where to remember that the negative-balance setting was turned on | A nullable timestamp column, re-attempted while empty | Review | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-55 | Should the original's complete name its upload (like Cancel's abort, Q-50)? | Yes | Review | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-56 | Hidden and direction characters in display names | Tidy them like titles (Q-43) | Review | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-57 | The payout line for a USD creator (P34) | "You'll get paid in US dollars (USD) to a US bank account." | Review | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-58 | `/api/stripe/onboarding` can be opened from another site or a sign-in link | Start a new setup only from inside ClipLease; reuse a re-read from the last 10 s | Review | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-59 | Better Auth's own /update-user lets a user set name and image | Close it | Review | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-60 | Poster objects that were PUT but never completed | Delete refused ones and sweep a video's strays older than 2 h 15 min | Review | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-61 | DataTable's stacked mode below lg | Leave it for P20, as planned | Review | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-62 | Opening the Source page of a post that is already licensable (the prompt's question 1) | The Source page shows "This post is already licensable." + "Open video" | P11 | answered (P11): the default |
| Q-63 | Where "‹ Back" goes on the Library File step | Back to the Library at every tier, no extra heading | P11 | answered (P11): option b, the default |
| Q-64 | Continue before "This file is the same video as the post" is ticked | Disabled, with "Tick the box to continue." | P11 | answered (P11): the default |
| Q-65 | New or proposed words ("Verifying…", "Post" / "Cover image", "Tick the box to continue.", "View on {Platform} (opens in a new tab)", "1 comment") | Use them as written | P11 | answered (P11): the default |
| Q-66 | On a computer, which side holds the upload part of the Library File step | Right, in one piece | P11 | answered (P11): the default |
| Q-67 | Back on the Source page after Reconnect | Only the connect error Banner on failure; nothing on success | P11 | answered (P11): the default |
| Q-68 | P11 runs 2–3× its 2-hour estimate | One step with a bigger budget | P11 | answered (P11): the default |
| Q-69 | A link verified on this video when a re-save or Check again hits a temporary platform error (the prompt's question 1) | Keep it verified; only the last-checked time moves | P11b | answered (P11b): the default |
| Q-70 | A link verified through an account that was disconnected, on a re-save | Keep it verified ("· no longer linked"); publishing asks for a reconnect (P14a) | P11b | answered (P11b): the default |
| Q-71 | Two Step 0 rg checks that only fail on code layout | Continue; show each passing with an equivalent search | P11b | answered (P11b): the default |
| Q-72 | P11b runs well past 2× its 105-minute estimate | One step with a bigger budget | P11b | answered (P11b): the default |
| Q-73 | Saving the Library source row's "Show buyers this link" Switch (the prompt's question 1) | An optional `sourceShowLink` on `saveVideoPostings` sets `show_link` on the source row only | P11c | answered (P11c): the default |
| Q-74 | Coming back to Where posted from Link / Reconnect {Platform} (the prompt's question 2) | Check that platform's `no_account` / `check_failed` links again once, each showing "Checking the link…"; `?error=` shows the connect error Banner | P11c | answered (P11c): the default |
| Q-75 | Same-video captions and the missing-tick error (the prompt's question 3) | "Post" for one, "{Platform} post" for several, "Cover image", cover first; "Confirm these posts show this same video" under the box (Continue stays enabled) | P11c | answered (P11c): the default |
| Q-76 | The article in "This is a {Platform} link. Move it there?" and the warning with several posts | "an" before Instagram and X; the warning names the post ("…but the {Platform} post is…") when several are verified (proposed) | P11c | answered (P11c): the default |
| Q-77 | Does replacing the original clear the same-video tick? | Yes: a finished Replace clears `same_video_confirmed_at` | P11c | answered (P11c): the default |
| Q-78 | Where the two cross-creator refusals show | Under the refused link (the errors carry its row index); nothing saved | P11c | answered (P11c): the default |
| Q-79 | "Add another link" on the Other card | A new name + link pair: each Other posting has its own name | P11c | answered (P11c): the default |
| Q-80 | Back and the heading on Where posted from tablet up | "‹ Back" → File at every tier, no visible heading | P11c | answered (P11c): the default |
| Q-81 | Deleting the File-step tick's files | Delete them, moving every still-valid test into the new step's tests | P11c | answered (P11c): the default |
| Q-82 | A time limit for a save's link checks (the review: 12 checks one by one under the step's 60 s action limit) | One 45 s budget for all of a save's checks (Check again its own); links not reached in time save as "We couldn't check this right now." with Check again, and everything else is saved | Review (P10–P11) | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-83 | Unticking a stored "These posts show this same video" | Takes the confirmation back: Continue clears it and asks "Confirm these posts show this same video" until it is ticked again | Review (P10–P11) | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-84 | A cooldown on checking the same link | A link checked in the last 30 s keeps its last result for Check again and Continue; Check again sooner shows "Checked a moment ago. Try again in a few seconds." (proposed) | Review (P10–P11) | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-85 | Telling two verified posts on one platform apart in the length warning | Name the account: "This file is 0:14 but the TikTok post on @maya.films is 0:38. Make sure it's the same video." (proposed) | Review (P10–P11) | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-86 | Where the "Show buyers this link" rule lives | The whole rule in the server's save (no database change); the browser posts the Switch only once turned; a choice follows the post, not the field | Review (P10–P11) | answered (Review): the default |
| Q-87 | Where the integration and e2e tests get their database (the Neon test branch over the network made `test:int` take 10.5 min) | A local Postgres 18 in Docker (Docker Desktop since Q-89; `bun run test:db`, `.env.test.local`); Neon keeps `dev` and stays the fallback | Test-speed review | answered (Tests): option b |
| Q-88 | What Step 0 re-runs of the previous prompt's checks | Typecheck, lint, token lint and unit always; integration and e2e only when the files differ from the last green `bun run check:full` (then the integration tests the changes reach and the prompt's e2e specs); every Step 4 still ends with the full suite | Test-speed review | answered (Tests): option b |
| Q-89 | Which Docker runs the test database once Docker Desktop is installed next to colima | Docker Desktop: colima stopped and left installed; `bun run test:db` opens Docker Desktop when Docker isn't up | Test-speed review | answered (Tests): option a |

## Prompt map

`docs/07-decisions.md` §3.2 numbers its prompts differently from this kit (P00–P21 plus H1–H10). Use this table to translate
any "Pxx" or "Hx" you meet in docs 01–07. The kit's `prompts/` files are the build order (`prompts/INDEX.md`).

| 07 §3.2 | This kit |
|---|---|
| P00 Scaffold + compat smoke | P00 (scaffold, pins, smoke) + P01 (tokens, `app-name.ts`, `env.ts`) |
| P01 Database | P03 |
| P02 Auth | P04 |
| P03 Shell + primitives | P02 |
| P04 Onboarding + creator page | P05 (also the 18+ checkbox, fake payouts, the payments registry, the CAD fact line, time zone); P05b (teens and guardians) is Later |
| P05 Social core | P06 (OAuth plumbing, mock) + P08 (Connections) |
| P06 Sync + Library | P07 (sync engine) + P09 (Library) |
| P07 Pricing | P12 |

| P09 Make licensable II | P11b + P11c (postings, Where posted) + P13 (Rights, Review) + P14a + P14b (publish, share, video page) |
